Wait, e4 is not diagonally adjacent to e3.
e4 is directly above e3.
Pawns capture diagonally!
Ok, that explains it. Pawns move forward and capture diagonally.
Black pawns move from rank 7 down to rank 1.
So a pawn at e4 moves to e3.
But there is a piece at e3 (the white rook).
Pawns cannot move forward if the square is occupied.
They can only capture diagonally.
So the pawn at e4 is blocked by the rook at e3.
That's why e4e3 is not a legal move.
